# Skills · P · part 271

1,195 entries, pwn to pybind-rollout-execution. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **pwn**: Binary exploitation for CTFs — stack ROP, format strings, heap, libc ([HeyMeMukul/AI-CTF-MACHINE](https://github.com/HeyMeMukul/AI-CTF-MACHINE/tree/HEAD/skills/pwn) · ★1)
- **pwn**: Solve authorized binary exploitation CTF challenges with checksec, debugging, and pwntools. ([Xh12321/ctftool-1.0](https://github.com/Xh12321/ctftool-1.0/tree/HEAD/agents/pwn))
- **PWN Dynamic Analysis**: This skill should be used when the user asks to "debug binary", "find offset", "calculate padding", "use gdb", "attach debugger", "crash analysis", "cyclic pattern", "test exploit", "bad characters", "find bad chars", or needs to perform runtime analysis of a binary. Provides methodology for dynamic debugging with GDB/pwndbg including bad character detection. ([allsmog/pwn-claude-plugin](https://github.com/allsmog/pwn-claude-plugin/tree/HEAD/pwn-htb/skills/dynamic-analysis) · ★1)
- **PWN Environment Setup**: This skill should be used when the user asks to "setup pwn environment", "install pwntools", "check pwn tools", "verify exploitation tools", "create exploit workspace", "initialize pwn project", or needs to verify binary exploitation tool availability. Provides guidance for detecting, installing, and configuring pwn tools. ([allsmog/pwn-claude-plugin](https://github.com/allsmog/pwn-claude-plugin/tree/HEAD/pwn-htb/skills/environment-setup) · ★1)
- **PWN Exploit Development**: This skill should be used when the user asks to "write exploit", "build payload", "create rop chain", "exploit binary", "pwn challenge", "get shell", "ret2libc", "bypass protection", or needs guidance on constructing exploitation payloads. Provides protection-aware exploitation techniques and payload construction. ([allsmog/pwn-claude-plugin](https://github.com/allsmog/pwn-claude-plugin/tree/HEAD/pwn-htb/skills/exploit-development) · ★1)
- **PWN Reconnaissance**: This skill should be used when the user asks to "analyze binary", "run checksec", "check binary protections", "identify architecture", "find strings in binary", "extract symbols", "recon binary", or needs initial binary analysis before exploitation. Provides methodology for the reconnaissance phase of binary exploitation. ([allsmog/pwn-claude-plugin](https://github.com/allsmog/pwn-claude-plugin/tree/HEAD/pwn-htb/skills/reconnaissance) · ★1)
- **PWN Static Analysis**: This skill should be used when the user asks to "disassemble binary", "analyze functions", "find vulnerability", "reverse engineer", "identify buffer size", "find dangerous functions", "analyze stack layout", or needs to perform static code analysis on a binary. Provides methodology for identifying vulnerabilities through disassembly. ([allsmog/pwn-claude-plugin](https://github.com/allsmog/pwn-claude-plugin/tree/HEAD/pwn-htb/skills/static-analysis) · ★1)

- **pwn-chain**: 从逆向走到可用利用 (Working Exploit) 的全链路工程化方法。 适用场景：拿到了二进制 + 漏洞点 + 目标环境，需要写出一个能稳定打通的 exploit（不是只能本地复现一下、远程一打就崩的脚本）。 覆盖三大方向：栈溢出 / 堆利用 / 内核 pwn。强调"CTF 本地通 → 真实远程稳定打通"的工程差距：libc 版本错配、堆喷射时序、SMEP/SMAP/KASLR、栈对齐、远程缓冲。 核心工具链：pwntools + GEF/pwndbg + ROPgadget/Ropper + one_gadget + libc-database + qemu-system 内核调试。 触发关键词：pwn、栈溢出、堆溢出、ROP、ret2libc、ret2csu、one_gadget、libc-database、堆利用、tcache、fastbin、unsorted bin、kernel pwn、kROP、SMEP、SMAP、KASLR、modprobe_path、pwntools、GEF、pwndbg。 ([zhaoxuya520/reverse-skill](https://github.com/zhaoxuya520/reverse-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/pwn-chain) · ★45k · 1.5k installs · 99 copies)
- **pwn-chain**: Full engineering workflow from reversing to a Working Exploit. Applicable scenario: you have a binary + a vulnerability point + a target environment, and need to write an exploit that lands reliably (not just something that works locally but crashes the moment you point it at remote). Covers three main directions: stack overflow / heap exploitation / kernel pwn. Emphasizes the engineering gap between "works locally in CTF" and "reliably lands against a real remote": libc version mismatches, heap spray timing, SMEP/SMAP/KASLR, stack alignment, remote buffering. - **pwn-chain**: 从漏洞点到可用利用的全链路工程化方法（working exploit / pwn chain）。能力：覆盖栈溢出、堆利用与内核 pwn 三大方向，把 CTF 本地能通的脚本打磨成远程稳定打通的 exploit，处理 libc 版本错配、堆喷射时序、SMEP/SMAP/KASLR、栈对齐与远程缓冲差异。适用场景：拿到二进制加漏洞点需要写 exploit、CTF 本地通但远程打不通、真实目标内存损坏漏洞要构造 RCE、Linux 内核驱动 ioctl bug 提权。Use when 用户提到 pwn、栈溢出、堆溢出、ROP、ret2libc、ret2csu、one_gadget、libc-database、堆利用、tcache、fastbin、unsorted bin、kernel pwn、kROP、SMEP、SMAP、KASLR、modprobe_path、pwntools、GEF、pwndbg，或提供 ELF、so、libc、vmlinux、ko 二进制与 core dump、远程 nc 地址。交付物：可复现的 pwntools exploit、连续多次的成功率验证记录与利用链说明。 ([lushun2014/lushibo-skills](https://github.com/lushun2014/lushibo-skills/tree/HEAD/pwn-chain) · ★5)

- **pwn-fsop**: Focused skill for FILE structure exploitation and glibc I/O based endings. Use this when heap primitives are strong enough and classic targets are blocked or inferior. ([ks2n/agent-skills](https://github.com/ks2n/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/pwn-fsop))
- **pwn-fundamentals**: Use when you need to exploit a classic stack buffer overflow on an authorized target, such as a CTF pwn challenge or a lab binary you have permission to test. Covers binary triage with checksec, finding the crash offset with cyclic patterns, hijacking control flow to a win function, leaking addresses to defeat ASLR, building a ret2libc exploit, and using one_gadget shortcuts, all with pwntools 4.13 and GDB 15.x. Use when a binary reads attacker-controlled input into a fixed-size stack buffer, or when you are learning binary exploitation from the ground up. ([mizazhaider-ceh/AgentArsenal](https://github.com/mizazhaider-ceh/AgentArsenal/tree/HEAD/skills/binary/pwn-fundamentals))
- **pwn-glibc-tcache-poisoning**: Use when solving CTF pwn challenges with glibc heap bugs, tcache dup/poisoning, use-after-free, double free, safe-linking leaks, malloc/free hooks, or arbitrary allocation primitives. Do not use for non-glibc allocators or pure stack overflows. ([loegaire/CTF_Journal](https://github.com/loegaire/CTF_Journal/tree/HEAD/writeups/ctf_skill_research/staging_skills/pwn-glibc-tcache-poisoning) · ★2)

- **pwn-heap-techniques**: Deep heap exploitation skill for ptmalloc-based Linux challenges. - **pwn-request**: Use when hunting Pwn Request vulnerabilities where pull_request_target workflows checkout attacker-controlled PR code and execute it in a privileged context with access to repository secrets. Trigger on: "pwn request", "pull_request_target", "checkout PR head", "npm install in CI", "lifecycle scripts in CI", "preinstall script", "postinstall script", "package.json scripts CI", "npm ci ignore-scripts false", "actions/checkout ref pull request head sha", privileged workflow running PR code, "Gato-X", supply chain via PR lifecycle scripts. ([ShulkwiSEC/bb-huge](https://github.com/ShulkwiSEC/bb-huge/tree/HEAD/skills/curated/pwn-request) · ★27 · 8 copies)
- **pwn-ret2libc-rop-chain**: Use when solving CTF pwn challenges with stack overflow, NX enabled, libc leak, ROP gadgets, ret2libc, ret2plt, ret2csu, one_gadget, SROP-like stack control, or staged return chains. Do not use for heap-only bugs or shellcode-only challenges. ([loegaire/CTF_Journal](https://github.com/loegaire/CTF_Journal/tree/HEAD/writeups/ctf_skill_research/staging_skills/pwn-ret2libc-rop-chain) · ★2)
